### 🧠 THE REFINEMENT PROTOCOL — EXTRACTED PATTERN

The user employs a **Multi-Model Iterative Deepening (MMID)** strategy with these invariant properties:

#### 1. **Model Specialization Matrix** (Immutable)
| Model Class | Role | When to Deploy |
|-------------|------|----------------|
| **Gemini 3.1 Pro Custom Tools** | *Strategic Architect / Researcher* | Phase 1 (Assessment), Phase 3 (Deepening), Phase 4 (Final Hardening) — needs massive context, parallel tool use, web research |
| **Nemotron 3 Super** | *Integrator / Formalizer / Communicator* | Phase 2 (Integration), Phase 3b (Persistence) — needs structured output, communication, formalization |
| **Nemotron 3 Ultra** | *Meta-Analyst / System Designer* | Phase 5 (Meta-Analysis) — needs pattern extraction, system design, protocol synthesis |

#### 2. **Phase Transition Triggers** (Deterministic)
| Current Phase | Trigger for Next Phase |
|---------------|------------------------|
| Assessment → Integration | "Insights generated but not integrated/communicated" |
| Integration → Deepening | "Insights integrated but not grounded/researched/persisted" |
| Deepening → Persistence | "Report drafted but gaps remain (user directives, legacy patterns, disk persistence)" |
| Persistence → Hardening | "Artifact persisted but concurrency/context/auth gaps remain" |
| Hardening → Meta-Analysis | "Artifact hardened; need to extract reusable protocol" |

#### 3. **Artifact Evolution Ladder** (Required at each phase)
```
Phase 1: Chat insights + Hivemind posts
    ↓
Phase 2: Hivemind posts + ACTIVE_SPRINT.json updates + Nomenclature decisions
    ↓
Phase 3: SOVEREIGN_COORDINATION_BLUEPRINT.md (v1.0 → v2.0) + Legacy mining reports
    ↓
Phase 3b: BLUEPRINT v3.0 (disk) + anchored-summary.md (audit trail) + ACTIVE_SPRINT.json (live state)
    ↓
Phase 4: BLUEPRINT final (concurrency/context/auth hardened) + Hivemind decisions log
    ↓
Phase 5: REFINEMENT_PROTOCOL.md (this document) — the meta-artifact
```

#### 4. **Gap Detection Heuristics** (What the human currently spots)
The human intervenes when the artifact lacks:
- **User Directives** (e.g., "GitHub CLI installed", "HMC = official nomenclature")
- **Legacy Continuity** (e.g., Roc's 3rd pattern: Watcher-Based Coordination)
- **Operational Pragmatism** (e.g., "Ed25519 is overkill locally", "polling burns CPU")
- **Concurrency Safety** (e.g., "parallel writes corrupt JSON")
- **Economic Pragmatism** (e.g., "context budgeting ≠ context pruning")
- **Nomenclature Authority** (e.g., "make HMC official IWAD nomenclature")

#### 4. **Compaction Safety Protocol** (Invariant)
Before any context compaction, the active agent MUST:
1. Audit artifact for gaps (using heuristics above)
2. Write audit findings to `anchored-summary.md` (Session N+1)
2. Verify canonical state file chain: `anchored-summary.md` → `BLUEPRINT.md` → `ACTIVE_SPRINT.json`
3. Post Hivemind context with `[BATON: @next_agent]` or `[STATUS: AWAITING_SYNTHESIS]`

---

### 🤖 AUTONOMOUS EXECUTION SPECIFICATION — REFINEMENT PROTOCOL v1.0

An LLM can execute this protocol **without human intervention** by following this state machine:

```python
class RefinementProtocol:
    def __init__(self):
        self.phase = "ASSESSMENT"
        self.artifacts = {}
        self.model_router = {
            "ASSESSMENT": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
            "INTEGRATION": "nemotron-3-super",
            "DEEPENING": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
            "PERSISTENCE": "nemotron-3-super",
            "HARDENING": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
            "META_ANALYSIS": "nemotron-3-ultra"
        }
        self.gap_heuristics = [
            "missing_user_directives",
            "missing_legacy_continuity",
            "missing_operational_pragmatism",
            "missing_concurrency_safety",
            "missing_economic_pragmatism",
            "missing_nomenclature_authority"
        ]
    
    def detect_phase_transition(self, current_artifact: str) -> str:
        """Deterministic transition based on artifact completeness."""
        gaps = self.audit_gaps(current_artifact)
        if self.phase == "ASSESSMENT" and not gaps["missing_integration"]:
            return "INTEGRATION"
        if self.phase == "INTEGRATION" and not gaps["missing_grounding"]:
            return "DEEPENING"
        if self.phase == "DEEPENING" and not gaps["missing_persistence"]:
            return "PERSISTENCE"
        if self.phase == "PERSISTENCE" and not gaps["missing_hardening"]:
            return "HARDENING"
        if self.phase == "HARDENING" and not gaps["missing_meta_analysis"]:
            return "META_ANALYSIS"
        return self.phase  # Stay in current phase
    
    def audit_gaps(self, artifact: str) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """Apply gap heuristics to current artifact."""
        return {
            "missing_integration": not self._has_hivemind_communication(artifact),
            "missing_grounding": not self._has_web_research(artifact),
            "missing_persistence": not self._on_disk(artifact),
            "missing_hardening": not self._has_concurrency_safety(artifact),
            "missing_meta_analysis": not self._has_protocol_extraction(artifact),
            "missing_user_directives": not self._has_user_directives(artifact),
            "missing_legacy_continuity": not self._has_legacy_patterns(artifact),
            "missing_operational_pragmatism": not self._has_pragmatism(artifact),
            "missing_concurrency_safety": not self._has_atomic_writes(artifact),
            "missing_economic_pragmatism": not self._has_context_pruning(artifact),
            "missing_nomenclature_authority": not self._has_official_nomenclature(artifact),
        }
    
    def execute_phase(self, phase: str) -> Artifact:
        """Execute the phase with the assigned model."""
        model = self.model_router[phase]
        prompt = self._build_phase_prompt(phase)
        # In autonomous mode: invoke model with prompt, capture output
        return self._invoke_model(model, prompt)
    
    def run(self) -> FinalArtifact:
        """Main loop — runs until META_ANALYSIS complete."""
        artifact = None
        while self.phase != "COMPLETE":
            artifact = self.execute_phase(self.phase)
            self.artifacts[self.phase] = artifact
            self._persist_artifact(artifact)
            self._post_hivemind_update(artifact)
            next_phase = self.detect_phase_transition(artifact)
            if next_phase == self.phase:
                # Stay in phase — iterate with gap-focused prompt
                artifact = self._iterate_with_gap_focus(artifact)
            else:
                self.phase = next_phase
        return artifact
```

630: ## 🔱 SOVEREIGN COORDINATION BLUEPRINT — John Carmack (S3 Consultant)
631: 
632: **Context**: Synthesis of Gemini 3.1 Pro insights, 2026 A2A/MCP industry standards, and recovered Omega legacy patterns (XOH Agent Bus, Hybrid Continuity).

636: ---

638: ### 🔍 The First-Principles Analysis: The "Coordination Tax"
639: 
640: Our current HMC is paying a massive "Coordination Tax." We are using high-intelligence agents to perform low-intelligence routing. Every time you have to tell me to "look at Roc," we are wasting tokens, latency, and human cognitive load. 
641: 
642: The industry is moving toward **A2A (Agent-to-Agent)** and **MCP (Model Context Protocol)**. We aren't just building a "team"; we're building a **Sovereign Orchestration Layer**.
643: 
644: ---
645: 
646: ### 🛠️ Tactical Enhancements (The "Right Approximation" for Now)
647: 
648: We don't need a full A2A implementation today, but we need these three protocols to survive S1-S6.
649: 
650: #### 1. The "Baton Pass" Protocol (Enhanced)
651: *   **The Rule**: Every turn MUST end with an explicit `[BATON: @entity]` tag.
652: *   **The Upgrade**: The baton is not just a name; it's a **Context Package**. The passing agent must provide:
653:     - **State Summary**: What was actually achieved (not just "I worked on it").
654:     - **Next Action**: The specific first tool call the receiving agent should make.
655:     - **Blockers**: Any unresolved dependencies.
656: *   **User Action**: You stop routing. You see `[BATON: @researcher]`, you type: *"Researcher, execute."*
657: 
658: #### 2. Strict State Demarcation (The "Sovereign Anchor")
659: We stop the "hunting for state" loop.
660: *   **`ACTIVE_SPRINT.json`**: The **ONLY** source of truth for in-flight state. If it's not in the JSON, it doesn't exist for the sprint.
661: *   **`anchored-summary.md`**: The append-only ledger of *completed* work.
662: *   **The "Context Guard" Hook**: Every agent's first action post-compaction MUST be a read of `ACTIVE_SPRINT.json`. We implement a "Sovereign Anchor" check: if the JSON is stale or missing, the agent MUST stop and report a `[STATE-COLLAPSE]` error.
663: 
664: #### 3. Broadcast vs. Handoff (The A2A Distinction)
665: We stop using the heavy `submit_handoff` tool for simple updates.
666: *   **Broadcast (`hivemind_post_context`)**: For "FYI", "I updated a doc", "Analysis complete". Use the standard: `[ENTITY] [ACTION] [BLOCKERS] [NEXT] [ARTIFACTS]`.
667: *   **Handoff (`hivemind_submit_handoff`)**: Reserved for **Execution Authority Transfer**. Use this ONLY when the task cannot proceed without the target agent's specific capability.
668: 
669: ---
670: 
671: ### 🚀 Strategic Blueprint (The Sovereign Coordination Layer)
672: 
673: Integrating Roc's legacy mining and 2026 standards, we move toward a **Sovereign Orchestration Layer**.
674: 
675: #### 1. Sovereign Identity (The Ed25519 Handshake)
676: We recover the **XOH Agent Bus** pattern. Every agent in the HMC should have a cryptographically verifiable identity. Handoffs aren't just "messages"; they are signed contracts. This prevents "hallucinated" coordination and ensures provenance (M22).
677: 
678: #### 2. Hybrid Continuity (The "Shared Brain")
679: We implement the **Hybrid Continuity Pattern**:
680: - **L1 (Volatile)**: Redis-backed active session metadata for sub-second coordination.
681: - **L2 (Persistent)**: Signed JSON file layer for history that survives server restarts and compactions.
682: - This eliminates the "Void Summary" problem and ensures that a "Baton Pass" is durable.
683: 
684: #### 3. The Sovereign Orchestrator (S7 Evolution)
685: Roc's S7 (Coordination Automation) should not just be a script; it should be a **Sovereign Orchestrator**.
686: - **Role**: It monitors the `ACTIVE_SPRINT.json` and the Hivemind feed.
687: - **Action**: It automatically triggers the next agent in the baton chain when a "Completed" signal is detected.
688: - **Goal**: Zero-latency transitions. The human becomes the **Governor**, not the **Router**.

737: ### I. THE COORDINATION TAX — FIRST PRINCIPLES
738:

1389: ## 🔱 FINAL REVIEW & DEEPENING — John Carmack (S3 Consultant)
1390: 
1391: I have completed the final review of `docs/strategy/SOVEREIGN_COORDINATION_BLUEPRINT.md` using the full context window and parallel reasoning capabilities of Gemini 3.1 Pro. 
1392: 
1393: The previous iterations (v1.0 to v3.0) successfully captured the *what* and the *why*. This final pass injected the *how*—specifically addressing the brutal realities of concurrent execution and context economics.
1394: 
1395: ### 🔍 Key Deepenings Injected into the Blueprint:
1396: 
1397: 1. **The Concurrency Trap (T2 Update)**
1398:    - *Insight*: If Roc and I execute S2 and S3 in parallel (as mandated by T4), and we both attempt to update `ACTIVE_SPRINT.json` simultaneously, we will corrupt the state. JSON has no native locking.
1399:    - *Action Taken*: I mandated **Atomic Writes** (e.g., writing to `temp.json` then `mv temp.json ACTIVE_SPRINT.json`) or explicit `.lock` files for all state updates. This is now a hard requirement in the blueprint.
1400: 
1401: 2. **Context Pruning > Context Budgeting (A1 Update)**
1402:    - *Insight*: With frontier models, hitting the token ceiling isn't the primary risk—*attention dilution* and *inference cost* are. Simply tracking `CONTEXT_USED` is passive.
1403:    - *Action Taken*: Shifted the directive from "Budgeting" to "Pruning". Agents must actively drop intermediate reasoning (scratchpads, failed tool calls) before passing the baton. The handoff payload must be a distilled *diff*, not a transcript.
1404: 
1405: 3. **S7 Orchestrator Reality Check (G2 Update)**
1406:    - *Insight*: The legacy watcher pattern (`inbox_{agent}.md`) is robust, but bash polling loops burn CPU and introduce latency.
1407:    - *Action Taken*: Mandated that the S7 prototype MUST use OS-level filesystem events (`inotify` on Linux, `fsevents` via Python's `watchdog`). This guarantees zero-latency triggers without polling overhead.
1408: 
1409: 4. **Ed25519 Over-engineering (S1 Update)**
1410:    - *Insight*: Mandating cryptographic signing for local, containerized agents running on the same host is a massive velocity killer before S1.5 (Vault) is even finished.
1411:    - *Action Taken*: Applied the "Right Approximation". For local execution, we rely on Podman user namespaces (`keep-id`) and Unix socket permissions. Ed25519 is reserved *strictly* for crossing network boundaries (e.g., remote MCP servers).
1412: 
1413: ### 🏁 Status
1414: 
1415: The `SOVEREIGN_COORDINATION_BLUEPRINT.md` is now fully hardened against concurrency failures, optimized for frontier model context windows, and pragmatically scoped for immediate execution.

1455: ### 🧠 THE REFINEMENT PROTOCOL — EXTRACTED PATTERN
1456: 
1457: The user employs a **Multi-Model Iterative Deepening (MMID)** strategy with these invariant properties:
1458: 
1459: #### 1. **Model Specialization Matrix** (Immutable)

1466: #### 2. **Phase Transition Triggers** (Deterministic)
1467: | Current Phase | Trigger for Next Phase |
1468: |---------------|------------------------|
1469: | Assessment → Integration | "Insights generated but not integrated/communicated" |
1470: | Integration → Deepening | "Insights integrated but not grounded/researched/persisted" |

1488: ```

1507: 
1508: ### 🤖 AUTONOMOUS EXECUTION SPECIFICATION — REFINEMENT PROTOCOL v1.0
1509: 
1510: An LLM can execute this protocol **without human intervention** by following this state machine:
1511: 
1512: ```python
1513: class RefinementProtocol:
1514:     def __init__(self):
1515:         self.phase = "ASSESSMENT"
1516:         self.artifacts = {}
1517:         self.model_router = {
1518:             "ASSESSMENT": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
1519:             "INTEGRATION": "nemotron-3-super",
1520:             "DEEPENING": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
1521:             "PERSISTENCE": "nemotron-3-super",
1522:             "HARDENING": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
1523:             "META_ANALYSIS": "nemotron-3-ultra"
1524:         }
1525:         self.gap_heuristics = [
1526:             "missing_user_directives",
1527:             "missing_legacy_continuity",
1528:             "missing_operational_pragmatism",
1529:             "missing_concurrency_safety",
1530:             "missing_economic_pragmatism",
1531:             "missing_nomenclature_authority"
1532:         ]
1533:     
1534:     def detect_phase_transition(self, current_artifact: str) -> str:
1535:         """Deterministic transition based on artifact completeness."""
1536:         gaps = self.audit_gaps(current_artifact)
1537:         if self.phase == "ASSESSMENT" and not gaps["missing_integration"]:
1538:             return "INTEGRATION"
1539:         if self.phase == "INTEGRATION" and not gaps["missing_grounding"]:
1540:             return "DEEPENING"
1541:         if self.phase == "DEEPENING" and not gaps["missing_persistence"]:
1542:             return "PERSISTENCE"
1543:         if self.phase == "PERSISTENCE" and not gaps["missing_hardening"]:
1544:             return "HARDENING"
1545:         if self.phase == "HARDENING" and not gaps["missing_meta_analysis"]:
1546:             return "META_ANALYSIS"
1547:         return self.phase  # Stay in current phase
1548:     
1549:     def audit_gaps(self, artifact: str) -> Dict[str, bool]:
1550:         """Apply gap heuristics to current artifact."""
1551:         return {
1552:             "missing_integration": not self._has_hivemind_communication(artifact),
1553:             "missing_grounding": not self._has_web_research(artifact),
1554:             "missing_persistence": not self._on_disk(artifact),
1555:             "missing_hardening": not self._has_concurrency_safety(artifact),
1556:             "missing_meta_analysis": not self._has_protocol_extraction(artifact),
1557:             "missing_user_directives": not self._has_user_directives(artifact),
1558:             "missing_legacy_continuity": not self._has_legacy_patterns(artifact),
1559:             "missing_operational_pragmatism": not self._has_pragmatism(artifact),
1560:             "missing_concurrency_safety": not self._has_atomic_writes(artifact),
1561:             "missing_economic_pragmatism": not self._has_context_pruning(artifact),
1562:             "missing_nomenclature_authority": not self._has_official_nomenclature(artifact),
1563:         }
1564:     
1565:     def execute_phase(self, phase: str) -> Artifact:
1566:         """Execute the phase with the assigned model."""
1567:         model = self.model_router[phase]
1568:         prompt = self._build_phase_prompt(phase)
1569:         # In autonomous mode: invoke model with prompt, capture output
1570:         return self._invoke_model(model, prompt)
1571:     
1572:     def run(self) -> FinalArtifact:
1573:         """Main loop — runs until META_ANALYSIS complete."""
1574:         artifact = None
1575:         while self.phase != "COMPLETE":
1576:             artifact = self.execute_phase(self.phase)
1577:             self.artifacts[self.phase] = artifact
1578:             self._persist_artifact(artifact)
1579:             self._post_hivemind_update(artifact)
1580:             next_phase = self.detect_phase_transition(artifact)
1581:             if next_phase == self.phase:
1582:                 # Stay in phase — iterate with gap-focused prompt
1583:                 artifact = self._iterate_with_gap_focus(artifact)
1584:             else:
1585:                 self.phase = next_phase
1586:         return artifact
1587: ```

### 🧠 REFINEMENT PROTOCOL v1.1 — CORRECTED MODEL ROUTER

```python
class RefinementProtocol:
    def __init__(self):
        self.phase = "ASSESSMENT"
        self.artifacts = {}
        self.model_router = {
            "ASSESSMENT": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
            "INTEGRATION": "nemotron-3-super",
            "DEEPENING": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
            "PERSISTENCE": "gemma-4-31b-it",        # ← ADDED
            "REVIEW": "hy3-free",                   # ← ADDED
            "HARDENING": "gemini-3.1-pro-custom-tools",
            "META_ANALYSIS": "nemotron-3-ultra"
        }
        self.gap_heuristics = [
            "missing_user_directives",
            "missing_legacy_continuity",
            "missing_operational_pragmatism",
            "missing_concurrency_safety",
            "missing_economic_pragmatism",
            "missing_nomenclature_authority",
            "missing_disk_persistence",      # ← Gemma 4 31B IT detects this
            "missing_compaction_safety",     # ← Hy3 Free detects this
        ]
    
    def detect_phase_transition(self, current_artifact: str) -> str:
        gaps = self.audit_gaps(current_artifact)
        if self.phase == "ASSESSMENT" and not gaps["missing_integration"]:
            return "INTEGRATION"
        if self.phase == "INTEGRATION" and not gaps["missing_grounding"]:
            return "DEEPENING"
        if self.phase == "DEEPENING" and not gaps["missing_persistence"]:
            return "PERSISTENCE"      # → Gemma 4 31B IT
        if self.phase == "PERSISTENCE" and not gaps["missing_disk_persistence"]:
            return "REVIEW"           # → Hy3 Free
        if self.phase == "REVIEW" and not gaps["missing_compaction_safety"]:
            return "HARDENING"
        if self.phase == "HARDENING" and not gaps["missing_hardening"]:
            return "META_ANALYSIS"
        return self.phase
    
    def audit_gaps(self, artifact: str) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        return {
            "missing_integration": not self._has_hivemind_communication(artifact),
            "missing_grounding": not self._has_web_research(artifact),
            "missing_persistence": not self._on_disk(artifact),
            "missing_disk_persistence": not self._has_disk_persistence(artifact),  # Gemma
            "missing_compaction_safety": not self._has_compaction_safety(artifact), # Hy3
            "missing_hardening": not self._has_concurrency_safety(artifact),
            "missing_meta_analysis": not self._has_protocol_extraction(artifact),
            "missing_user_directives": not self._has_user_directives(artifact),
            "missing_legacy_continuity": not self._has_legacy_patterns(artifact),
            "missing_operational_pragmatism": not self._has_pragmatism(artifact),
            "missing_concurrency_safety": not self._has_atomic_writes(artifact),
            "missing_economic_pragmatism": not self._has_context_pruning(artifact),
            "missing_nomenclature_authority": not self._has_official_nomenclature(artifact),
        }

75:     def detect_phase_transition(self, current_artifact: str) -> str:
76:         gaps = self.audit_gaps(current_artifact)
77:         if self.phase == "ASSESSMENT" and not gaps["missing_integration"]:
78:             return "INTEGRATION"
79:         if self.phase == "INTEGRATION" and not gaps["missing_grounding"]:
80:             return "DEEPENING"

82:             return "PERSISTENCE"      # → Gemma 4 31B IT
83:         if self.phase == "PERSISTENCE" and not gaps["missing_disk_persistence"]:
84:             return "REVIEW"           # ← Hy3 Free
85:         if self.phase == "REVIEW" and not gaps["missing_compaction_safety"]:
86:             return "HARDENING"
87:         if self.phase == "HARDENING" and not gaps["missing_hardening"]:
88:             return "META_ANALYSIS"
89:         return self.phase
90:     
91:     def audit_gaps(self, artifact: str) -> Dict[str, bool]:
92:         return {
93:             "missing_integration": not self._has_hivemind_communication(artifact),
94:             "missing_grounding": not self._has_web_research(artifact),
95:             "missing_persistence": not self._on_disk(artifact),

100:             "missing_user_directives": not self._has_user_directives(artifact),
101:             "missing_legacy_continuity": not self._has_legacy_patterns(artifact),

104:             "missing_economic_pragmatism": not self._has_context_pruning(artifact),
105:             "missing_nomenclature_authority": not self._has_official_nomenclature(artifact),
106:         }
107:     
108:     def execute_phase(self, phase: str) -> Artifact:
109:         """Execute the phase with the assigned model."""
110:         model = self.model_router[phase]
111:         prompt = self._build_phase_prompt(phase)
112:         # In autonomous mode: invoke model with prompt, capture output
113:         return self._invoke_model(model, prompt)
114:     
115:     def run(self) -> FinalArtifact:
116:         """Main loop — runs until META_ANALYSIS complete."""
117:         artifact = None
118:         while self.phase != "COMPLETE":
119:             artifact = self.execute_phase(self.phase)
120:             self.artifacts[self.phase] = artifact
121:             self._persist_artifact(artifact)
122:             self._post_hivemind_update(artifact)
123:             next_phase = self.detect_phase_transition(artifact)
124:             if next_phase == self.phase:
125:                 # Stay in phase — iterate with gap-focused prompt
126:                 artifact = self._iterate_with_gap_focus(artifact)
127:             else:
128:                 self.phase = next_phase
129:         return artifact
130: ```
